# Pantry Foods

One entry per distinct ingredient string as it appears in a recipe's Ingredients
table (see `docs/RECIPE_FORMAT.md`) — **not** merged across wording variants,
per Holger's explicit choice (2026-07). Identical text reused verbatim across
recipes collapses into one entry with multiple "Used in" links; near-duplicate
wording (e.g. "Onion, diced" vs "Onion, finely diced") stays separate.

A few **stocked-only** entries exist too — pantry items recorded here on request
that aren't (yet) used in any recipe. They carry `"recipes":[]` and `"stocked":true`
in the data block, and their heading shows *"stocked staple — not yet used in a
recipe"* in place of a "Used in" list.

Cells naming two foods at once (e.g. "Salt and black pepper") are kept as a
single entry with both foods' profiles shown side by side (Holger's choice).

**Sourcing policy:** macros from original sources where possible — BWFO
(`docs/BWFO_GRAPHQL.md`, Holger's actual shop) checked first, USDA
(`docs/USDA_FDC.md`) as fallback. Micros from USDA / estimate / hybrid,
whichever fits. Every entry's source is cited so it can be verified or
corrected — gaps are marked `profile pending`, not papered over. A few
pending entries carry a stated reason (bad/missing source data found) rather
than being silently skipped.

Each entry carries a trailing ```json:pantry-data``` block —
machine-readable data for `webapp/scripts/build-index.mjs`. Not meant for
reading directly; the prose above it is the human-readable version of the
same data.

Generated + hand-curated across sessions; not auto-regenerated by
`build-index.mjs` (unlike recipe content) — edits here are the source of
truth for pantry nutrition data. Regenerated 2026-07-17 to pick up 5 recipes
and Shakshuka's Carob-Chipotle-Tamarind profile added concurrently by Holger.
6 stocked-only entries added the same day to cover `docs/PANTRY.md` staples
not yet used in any documented recipe (chia seeds, coriander seeds, lentils,
Medjool dates, banana blossom, alphabet egg noodle soup pasta).

**Coverage:** 228 of 513 entries researched
(219 single-food, 6 stocked-only, 3 compound-cell), 285 pending.
